Implementation of candidate evaluation:
```
def best_pair_move(p_arr, q_arr, a, g, w, max_delta):
    Gp = g[p_arr]; Gq = g[q_arr]
    diff = Gp[:,None] - Gq[None,:]
    mask = diff > 1e-12
    if not any return
    P = p_arr[:,None]; Q = q_arr[None,:]
    B = w[2*P] - 2*w[P+Q] + w[2*Q]
    Ap = a[p_arr][:,None]
    if max_delta finite: Ap = np.minimum(Ap, max_delta)
    # delta interval [0, Ap]
    absB = np.abs(B)
    delta = np.zeros_like(diff)
    pos = mask & (absB > 1e-14)
    # For B>0: unconstrained = diff/B; clamp
    d_star = diff[B? ] / B
    for B>0: delta = clip(d_star,0,Ap)
    for B<0: delta = Ap
    for B~0: delta = Ap (if diff>0 linear decreasing)
    val = -2*delta*diff + delta*delta*B
    # also consider maybe delta=0 no
    valid = val < -eps
    choose min val
```
But if B<0, delta=Ap may produce val? derivative negative throughout; val most negative at max δ. If Ap huge, val very negative. OK.
For B positive but d_star > Ap, choose Ap; if d_star <0 impossible since diff>0, B>0 -> >0. Good.
If B positive and d_star very small, choose small.
Threshold: Q magnitude maybe O(1) (since w sum 1, c avg1). Need tolerance e.g. -1e-12. Due roundoff, require val < -1e-10 maybe. We can pick if best_val < -1e-9.

After move:
```
a[p] -= delta; a[q] += delta; a[p]=max(0,...) due eps.
g += 2*delta*(w[q:q+n] - w[p:p+n])
# optional update a sum? no.
# For numerical, if a[p]<1e-15 set 0.
```

```
def make_weights(c, M, beta, mix, K):
    # Method: top-k logistic + tail uniform
    tau = M / beta  # if beta=10, tau=0.15
    logits = (c - M)/tau
    logits = np.clip(logits, -50, 0)
    w = np.exp(logits)
    # maybe add small uniform
    w += eps
    # symmetrize: w = 0.5*(w + w[::-1])
    w += mix/N_lags?
    w /= w.sum()
```

```
def run_lse_fista(a, max_time, beta_init, beta_growth, step, use_acc):
    S=sqrt(2n); normalize
    A=fft(a,N); c=ifft(A*A); M=c.max()
    t=1; y=a
    while time:
       p=softmax(c/tau)
       g=2corr(p,a)
       # line search on objective or LSE
       if accel: new t, y
       x=proj(y - step*g)
       recompute c_x? maybe only if step large.
       if obj_x > obj_y + tol or not finite: step = step*0.5; x=proj(y-step*g); else step*=1.1
       a=x; t update
       # periodic restarts if obj not improved.
```

2. `project_simplex(v, S)`:
```
def project_simplex(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(v)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.arange(1, n+1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = (css - S) / rho <= u
    idx = np.where(cond)[0]
    if len(idx)==0:
       return np.full(n, S/n, dtype=np.float64)
    r = idx.max()
    theta = (css[r] - S) / (r+1)
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    # correct sum due roundoff
    s=out.sum()
    if s > 0:
       out = out * (S/s)
    return out
```
But if v has negative huge, sorting ok. For many calls, precompute `rho`? Could pass. We can implement specialized with local variables. Use `np.argsort`? Sorting descending O(n log n). Could use `np.sort(v)[::-1]`. For n large, fine.

Optimization: In FISTA, projected gradient many times; sort cost. We can use an approximate projection? But exact simple maybe. Could use `scipy.optimize`? no.

We can reduce projection calls: Coordinate CD maintains sum, no projection needed except initial/random restarts. FISTA needs. Weighted CD no projection if sum preserved. So sort not too frequent.

3. `normalize_nonneg(a, S)`:
```
def normalize_nonneg(a, S):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12:
       a.fill(S/len(a))
    else:
       a*=S/s
    return a
```

4. `eval_c(a, cache)`? We'll have global N. For each n, N computed. We can write:
```
def compute_convolution(a, N=None):
   n=len(a)
   if N is None: N=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
   A=fft(a, N, workers=1?)
   c=np.real(ifft(A*A, workers=1))[:2*n-1]
   # clip tiny negative
   c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
   return c, A
```
Scipy fft has workers param? `scipy.fft.fft(a, n=N, workers=1)` supported. Use workers=1 to ensure 1 CPU? Might help. But sometimes overhead. Use `scipy.fft.fft(a, N)`.

7. `weighted_coordinate_descent(a, w, n, N, A, max_moves, deadline, abs_cap, q_tol, verbose)`:

Let's pseudo implement:
```
def optimize_sequence(a, S, n, N, deadline, label, strategies, min_budget=30):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a,S)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_M=np.inf; best_obj=np.inf
    c,A=compute_convolution(a,N); best_M=c.max()
    no_improve_iters=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
        # evaluate current
        c,A=compute_convolution(a,N); M=c.max()
        if M < best_M - 1e-12:
           best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M; print
           no_improve=0
        else:
           no_improve+=1
        if M <= target? continue? We can continue to polish.
        # choose next strategy cyclic; if no_improve > len(strategies), add noise
        strat = strategies[step_idx % len]
        w = make_weights(c,n,mode,beta,K,mix)
        # inner_time: maybe 30-120 seconds depending
        inner_deadline = min(deadline - 10, time.monotonic() + 60)
        a2 = weighted_cd(a,w,n,N,A,inner_deadline, ...)
        # maybe after inner do one projected gradient LSE step? no
        a = normalize_nonneg(a2,S)
        step_idx +=1
        # if strategy produced little Q improvement? weighted_cd returns count; if 0 and no_improve large, noise/restart
        if weighted_cd moves == 0 or M > best_M*1.02:
            if no_improve > len(strategies):
                # random perturbation from best
                a = project_simplex(best_a + rng.normal(0, noise, n), S)
            else:
                a = best_a.copy() # or continue
    return best_a,best_M
```

```
def exact_coordinate_descent(a, w, c, n, N, A, S, deadline, K=128, max_moves=1000, obj_tol=1e-10):
   # recompute g from w,a,A (A should match c)
   g=...
   current_M = c.max()
   for it in range(max_moves):
      if time > deadline: break
      select top Q candidates (val). Also maybe random.
      Evaluate top E (say 8) exact:
         for each p,q,delta_q:
             best_M_move = current_M
             best_d = None
             for d in [delta_q, 0.5,0.25,0.125]: if d<=a[p]: newM=move_new_max(c,a,p,q,d,current_M); if newM<best_M_move: ...
             if best_M_move < current_M - obj_tol:
                 # choose among all candidate deltas the one with min M, maybe tie Q
                 accept first with improvement, or best
      if accepted:
         update c,a,g
         current_M = newM
      else:
         # no exact improvement among top Q. Try random pairs? maybe.
         # If no improvement for many, break
      # if current_M improved, perhaps continue; if not break or after few no improve
```

Correct LSE projected gradient/FISTA:
```
def lse_gradient(a,A,c,tau):
   d=(c-c.max())/tau
   p=np.exp(d); p/=p.sum()
   W=fft(p,N)
   g=2*real(ifft(W*conj(A))[:n])
   return p,g
```
But if tau small, p concentrated, gradient like active. We can use surrogate CD instead; LSE FISTA likely redundant. But maybe a few global steps with LSE and larger step can escape. Let's implement simple accelerated projected gradient on LSE with backtracking, called for fixed time. It can use correct gradient.

Line search on LSE or objective? Since we want objective M, use M. We can accept if M improves or LSE improves. Use objective to avoid bad.

Pseudo:
```
def run_lse(a,S,n,N,deadline,beta=20,step=0.5):
   a=normalize
   y=a; t=1; best...
   while time:
      c,A=conv
      M=c.max()
      tau=M/beta
      p,g=lse
      x=project(y - step*g, S)
      # line search on M
      for bt in 5:
         c_x,A_x=conv(x)
         if c_x.max() <= M + 1e-9 or step <1e-6: break
         step*=0.5; x=project(y-step*g)
      # encourage accept even no improve? maybe if LSE lower. But if no accept, step small.
      # FISTA momentum:
      t_new=...
      y = x + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x-a)
      y=project(y,S) # ensure
      a=x
      t=t_new
      if no improve for 100: t=1; add noise
      step = min(step*1.05, 2.0)
```

Pseudo `optimize_sequence`:
```
def optimize_sequence(a, S, n, N, deadline, label, strategies=None):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a,S)
    c,A=compute_convolution(a,N)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_M=c.max()
    print(label initial best_M)
    step_idx=0
    no_improve=0
    last_M=best_M
    while time < deadline-10:
        if step_idx % (len(strategies)*?) ...
        c,A=compute_convolution(a,N)  # maybe after a changes
        M=c.max()
        if M < best_M - 1e-10:
            best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M; no_improve=0; print
        else:
            no_improve += 1
        # if stuck, reset/noise
        if no_improve > 12:
            # choose noise
            sigma = 0.01 * S / np.sqrt(n)
            a = project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0, sigma, n), S)
            no_improve = 0
            continue
        strat = strategies[step_idx % len(strategies)]
        w=make_weights(c, n, strat[0], strat[1], strat[2], strat[3])
        # surrogate time: maybe 20s + if no moves longer
        inner_deadline = min(deadline-5, time.monotonic() + inner_time)
        a2, moves = surrogate_cd(a,w,c,n,N,A,S,inner_deadline,K=128,max_moves=10000)
        a=normalize_nonneg(a2,S)
        # exact polish from a2 with same w
        c,A=compute_convolution(a,N)
        M=c.max()
        if M < best_M: update
        inner_deadline2 = min(deadline-5, time.monotonic()+ polish_time)
        a3, c3, M3, moves3 = exact_cd(a,w,c,n,N,A,S,inner_deadline2,K=96,max_moves=2000)
        a=normalize_nonneg(a3,S)
        c=c3; M=M3 (exact_cd maintains c)
        if M < best_M: update
        # If current M worse than best by >1%, reset to best
        if M > best_M * 1.01 and best_a is not None:
            # keep some progress? reset to best to focus
            a=best_a.copy()
        step_idx += 1
    return best_a,best_M
```

def normalize_nonneg(a, S):

def weighted_gradient(a, A, w, N):

Maybe `evaluate_candidate_pairs(src, tgt, a, g, w, abs_cap)` returns val matrix, delta matrix? Use for surrogate and exact. Let's write:
```
def pair_candidate_values(src, tgt, a, g, w, abs_cap):
    P = src[:, None]
    Q = tgt[None, :]
    Gp = g[src]
    Gq = g[tgt]
    diff = Gp[:, None] - Gq[None, :]
    mask = (diff > 1e-12) & (src[:, None] != tgt[None, :]) & (a[src][:, None] > 1e-14)
    if not np.any(mask): return None
    P = src[:, None]; Q = tgt[None, :]
    B = w[2*P] - 2.0*w[P + Q] + w[2*Q]
    Ap = np.minimum(a[src], abs_cap)[:, None]
    epsB=1e-14
    pos = mask & (B > epsB)
    neg = mask & (B < -epsB)
    zero = mask & ~(pos | neg)
    delta = np.zeros(diff.shape, dtype=np.float64)
    # For pos:
    if np.any(pos):
       dstar = diff[pos] / (2.0 * B[pos])
       dstar = np.maximum(dstar, 0.0)
       dstar = np.minimum(dstar, Ap[pos])
       delta[pos] = dstar
    if np.any(neg):
       delta[neg] = Ap[neg]
    if np.any(zero):
       delta[zero] = Ap[zero]
    # invalid mask false delta 0
    val = -delta * diff + delta * delta * B
    val[~mask] = np.inf
    return val, delta
```
But advanced indexing `diff[pos]` with boolean works. Need ensure Ap[pos] shape. Good.
If B negative, Ap may be > actual a[p]; we capped. If source mass zero, mask false.
`abs_cap` if 0 means no cap? We'll use positive.

def find_best_move(a, g, w, rng, K, abs_cap):
    n=len(a)
    K = min(K, n)
    candidates = []
    # top
    if K > 0:
      g_src = g.copy(); g_src[a <= 1e-12] = -np.inf
      if K >= n:
         src = np.where(g_src > -np.inf)[0]
      else:
         src = np.argpartition(g_src, n-K)[n-K:]
         src = src[np.isfinite(g_src[src])]
      if K >= n:
         tgt = np.arange(n)
      else:
         tgt = np.argpartition(g, K)[:K]
      if len(src)>0 and len(tgt)>0:
          vald = pair_candidate_values(src,tgt,a,g,w,abs_cap)
          if vald[0] is not None:
              flat = vald[0].ravel(); flat_delta = vald[1].ravel()
              finite = np.isfinite(flat)
              if np.any(finite):
                  idx = np.argmin(flat)
                  if flat[idx] < 0: return src[idx//len(tgt)]...
    # random
    nonzero=...
    R=min(64,n*2)
    if len(nonzero)>0:
       src = nonzero[rng.integers(0,len(nonzero),R)]
       tgt = rng.integers(0,n,R)
       vald=...
       choose min if <0
    return None
```

`surrogate_coordinate_descent` function signature:

def surrogate_coordinate_descent(a_in, w, c, n, N, A_in, S, deadline, rng, K=128, max_moves=20000, abs_cap_factor=50.0):
    a = a_in.astype(np.float64).copy()
    W = fft(w, N)
    # A should correspond to a_in; but if not, recompute? We'll assume.
    g = weighted_gradient_from_A(a,A_in,w,N) # need function taking A
    Q = float(np.dot(w,c))
    best_Q=Q
    moves=0
    last=0
    abs_cap = abs_cap_factor * (S / n)
    # if abs_cap <=0 no cap
    for it in range(max_moves):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
        mv = find_best_move(a,g,w,rng,K,abs_cap)
        if mv is None:
            if it - last > 200: break
            continue
        p,q,delta,val = mv
        if val < -1e-10:
            # apply
            if delta > a[p]: delta = a[p]
            if delta <=0: continue
            # update g before a? uses old a? g update independent of a; ok.
            g += 2.0*delta*(w[q:q+n] - w[p:p+n])
            old = a[p]
            newp = old - delta
            if newp < 0: newp=0; delta = old #? but a[q] should add old? If clip, adjust
            a[p]=newp
            a[q]+=delta
            Q += val
            best_Q = Q
            moves +=1
            last=it
            # Optional periodic recompute g
            if it % 5000 == 0:
               A_cur = fft(a,N)
               g = weighted_gradient_from_A(a,A_cur,w,N)
               # Q recompute? not easy without c; could compute c=fft... but skip.
        else:
            # no improvement
            if it - last > 100:
               break
    a = normalize_nonneg(a,S)
    return a, moves
```

def exact_coordinate_descent(a_in, w, c_in, n, N, A_in, S, deadline, rng, K=96, max_moves=2000, abs_cap_factor=50.0, obj_tol=1e-9):
    a = a_in.astype(np.float64).copy()
    c = c_in.astype(np.float64).copy()
    W = fft(w,N)
    g = gradient_from_A(a,A_in,w,N)
    current_M = float(c.max())
    moves=0
    last=0
    abs_cap = abs_cap_factor * (S/n)
    # precompute outside? no
    for it in range(max_moves):
       if time >= deadline: break
       # select top candidates for Q
       Kc = min(K,n)
       g_src = g.copy(); g_src[a <= 1e-12] = -np.inf
       if Kc >= n: src=...
       else: src=np.argpartition(g_src, n-Kc)[n-Kc:]; src=src[np.isfinite(g_src[src])]
       tgt=np.argpartition(g,Kc)[:Kc] if Kc<n else np.arange(n)
       mvlist = pair_candidate_values...
       if not finite: break
       # get top E candidates with finite val
       flat_val = val.ravel(); flat_delta=delta.ravel()
       finite = np.isfinite(flat_val) & (flat_val < -1e-12)
       if not any: maybe random exact? break
       fin_vals = np.where(finite, flat_val, np.inf)
       E=6
       if count > E: cand_idx = np.argpartition(fin_vals, E)[:E]
       else: cand_idx = np.where(finite)[0]
       best_candidate=None
       for idx in cand_idx:
           col=idx % len(tgt); row=idx // len(tgt)
           p=int(src[row]); q=int(tgt[col]); d0=float(delta matrix?)
           # test deltas
           best_new=current_M; best_d=None
           for frac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
               d=d0*frac
               if d<=0 or d>a[p]: continue
               newM=move_new_max(c,a,p,q,d,current_M)
               if newM < best_new - 1e-12:
                  best_new=newM; best_d=d
           if best_d is not None and best_new < current_M - obj_tol:
               if best_candidate is None or best_new < best_candidate[4]:
                   best_candidate=(p,q,best_d,best_new,None)
       if best_candidate is None:
           if it - last > 50: break
           continue
       p,q,d,newM=...
       # apply update c,g,a
       c[p:p+n] -= 2.0*d*a
       c[q:q+n] += 2.0*d*a
       c[2*p] += d*d
       c[p+q] -= 2.0*d*d
       c[2*q] += d*d
       g += 2.0*d*(w[q:q+n] - w[p:p+n])
       a[p] -= d; a[q] += d; if a[p]<0 set 0
       # clean small negatives in c? no
       current_M = newM
       moves +=1; last=it
       # Periodic recompute A/g/c to prevent drift? Maybe every 1000 moves:
       if it % 1000 == 0:
           c2,A2=compute_convolution(a,N)
           c=c2; g=gradient_from_A(a,A2,w,N); current_M=c.max()
    a=normalize_nonneg(a,S)
    # final c from a? If we return a, plus maybe c? For exact_cd return a,c,current_M. But if normalized at end, c stale. We can recompute final c before return.
    c,A=compute_convolution(a,N)
    return a,c,c.max(),moves
```

`move_new_max` implementation top-level:
```
def move_new_max(c, a, p, q, d, current_M):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    start = p if p < q else q
    end = (p if p > q else q) + n
    if end > L: end = L
    length = end - start
    if length <=0:
        return current_M
    tmp = c[start:end].copy()
    # Linear terms
    off = p - start
    if off >=0:
        stop = off + n
        if stop > length: stop = length
        tmp[off:stop] -= (2.0*d) * a[:stop-off]  # need a corresponding t-p
```
Wait slice assignment must align correctly. For p range, t from p to p+n-1 inclusive; local index = t-start = p-start + i where i=0..n-1. The term is -2d a[i] (since t-p=i). So `tmp[off_p : off_p+n] -= 2d * a` (full a). Since off_p+n ≤ end? If p=min? If p<q, start=p, off_p=0, off_p+n = n. end=q+n maybe ≥n yes. If p=q no. If p>q, start=q, off_p=p-q, off_p+n = p-q+n = end, within. So we can simply:
```
off_p = p - start; tmp[off_p:off_p+n] -= 2*d*a
off_q = q - start; tmp[off_q:off_q+n] += 2*d*a
```
No need stop because end = max(p,q)+n and off_min? Check p<q: start=p, off_q=q-p, off_q+n = q-p+n=end; ok. q<p: start=q, off_p=p-q, off_p+n=end; off_q=0, off_q+n=n ≤ end? end=p+n, p>q, so n ≤ p+n yes. Good.
Quadratics: local = idx-start; if 0<=local< length add.
Then outside:
```
outmax = -np.inf
if start > 0: outmax = max(outmax, np.max(c[:start]))
if end < L: outmax = max(outmax, np.max(c[end:]))
localmax = np.max(tmp)
return max(localmax, outmax)
```
If `current_M` unknown? We can ignore and compute full outside; no need pass current_M. Passing current_M could speed if we know max outside? But we compute. Could use current_M to avoid outside if interval covers all? If start==0 and end==L return tmp.max(). We'll compute. This becomes O(L) max plus shaves. Good.

Time management in `propose_candidate`:
```

    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    if model == "soft":
        beta=float(param)
        M=float(c.max())
        if beta <=0 or M <=0: base=np.ones(L)/L
        else:
           tau = max(1e-12, M / beta)
           scaled=(c-M)/tau
           scaled=np.clip(scaled, -40.0, 0.0)
           base=np.exp(scaled)
           s=base.sum()
           if s <=0: base = np.ones(L)/L
           else: base/=s
    elif model == "topfrac":
        frac=float(param)
        K = int(round(frac * (L-1)))
        K = max(4, K)
        K = min(K, max(4, (L-1)//2)) ? If L small.
        if K >= L: base = np.ones(L)/L
        else:
           idx=np.argpartition(c, L-K)[L-K:]
           base=np.zeros(L); base[idx]=1.0/K
    else: base uniform
    # sym
    base = 0.5*(base + base[::-1])
    # mix
    if mix and mix>0:
       w = (1.0 - mix)*base + mix*(1.0/L)
    else: w=base
    w=np.maximum(w,0)
    s=w.sum()
    if s <=0: w=np.ones(L)/L
    else: w/=s
    return w
```
If topfrac L small (n=2 L=3), K max4 min? Need handle. Use:
```
maxK = max(1, L//2)
K = max(1, min(maxK, K))
```
Good. If K=1, top one; sym base average of t and rev.

def surrogate_coordinate_descent(...):
   a=copy; W=fft(w,N); g=...
   moves=0; last=0
   for it in range(max_moves):
      if time>=deadline: break
      mv=find_best_move(...)
      if mv is None:
         if it-last > 100: break
         continue
      p,q,delta,val=mv
      if val < -1e-10:
          if delta > a[p]: delta=a[p]
          if delta <= 1e-15: continue
          g += 2*delta*(w[q:q+n]-w[p:p+n])
          newp=a[p]-delta
          if newp < 0: newp=0.0; delta=a[p] # adjust? if a[p] changed? use oldp
          a[p]=newp; a[q]+=delta
          moves+=1; last=it
          if it % 5000 ==0:
              A=fft(a,N)
              g=2*real(ifft(W*conj(A)))[:n]
          # optional if abs sum drift? no
   a=normalize_nonneg(a,S)
   return a, moves
```

`exact_coordinate_descent`: We need `pair_candidate_values` for top selection. Use K maybe 64. It returns val matrix. Instead of using `find_best_move` (which would choose one), we need top E. Implement top selection inline:
```
def exact_coordinate_descent(...):
   a=copy; c=copy; W=fft(w,N); g=...
   current_M=float(c.max())
   moves=0; last=0
   for it in range(max_moves):
       if time>=deadline: break
       nonzero=...
       Kc=min(K,len(nonzero)); if Kc==0 break
       if Kc>=len(nonzero): src=nonzero
       else: src=nonzero[argpartition(g[nonzero], len(g[nonzero])-Kc)[len-Kc:]]
       Ktgt=min(K,n); if Ktgt>=n tgt=all else argpartition g
       val,delta=pair_candidate_values(...)
       flat=val.ravel(); flat_d=delta.ravel()
       finite=np.isfinite(flat) & (flat < -1e-12)
       if not any:
           if it-last>50: break
           continue
       if finite.sum() > 6:
           cand_idx = np.argpartition(np.where(finite, flat, np.inf), 6)[:6]
       else: cand_idx=np.where(finite)[0]
       best=None
       for idx in cand_idx:
           row=idx//len(tgt); col=idx%len(tgt); p=int(src[row]); q=int(tgt[col]); d0=float(flat_d[idx])
           if d0 <=0: continue
           # test fractions
           dlist=[]
           for frac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
              d=d0*frac
              if 0<d<=a[p]: dlist.append(d)
           # also if d0 > a[p] and a[p]>0 include a[p]
           best_new=current_M
           for d in dlist:
              newM=move_new_max(c,a,p,q,d)
              if newM < best_new - 1e-12:
                  best_new=newM; best_d=d
           if best_d is not None and best_new < current_M - obj_tol:
               if best is None or best_new < best[3]: best=(p,q,best_d,best_new)
       if best is None:
           if it-last>20: break
           continue
       p,q,d,newM=best
       # update c/g/a
       # before update, ensure a[p]>=d
       if d > a[p]: d=a[p]
       lin = 2.0*d
       c[p:p+n] -= lin * a
       c[q:q+n] += lin * a
       d2=d*d
       c[2*p] += d2
       c[p+q] -= 2.0*d2
       c[2*q] += d2
       g += lin*(w[q:q+n]-w[p:p+n])
       a[p] -= d; a[q] += d
       if a[p] < 0: a[p]=0
       # Maybe clip c tiny negative? not
       current_M = newM
       moves +=1; last=it
       if it % 1000 == 0:
           c,A=compute_convolution(a,N)
           g=2*real(ifft(W*conj(A)))[:n]
           current_M=float(c.max())
   a=normalize_nonneg(a,S)
   c,A=compute_convolution(a,N)
   return a,c,float(c.max()),moves
```

def upsample_sequence(a, factor=2):
   n=len(a); if n==1: return normalize...

      a_new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a, left=a[0], right=a[-1])

```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    start_total = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    reserve = min(120.0, total_budget*0.05)
    main_deadline = start_total + max(30.0, total_budget - reserve)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a0 = load_start_sequence(rng)
    n = len(a0); S=np.sqrt(2.0*n); N=next_power_two(2*n-1)
    stage1_deadline = start_total + max(60.0, (main_deadline-start_total)*0.70)
    best_a,obj = optimize_sequence(a0,S,n,N,stage1_deadline,"stage1",rng)
    candidates = [(best_a,obj)]

       a_div = make_initial(n, rng, "random")
       if n > 4096: a_div = make_initial(n, rng, "chirp")? maybe one.
       div_deadline = min(main_deadline - 60, time.monotonic() + 120)

Now, `make_initial` methods. Fallback used only if `height_sequence_1` missing. Could create a mix of random and cosine to have broad spectrum:
```
def make_initial(n, rng, method="mixed"):
   S=np.sqrt(2*n)
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
   if method == "random": a = rng.random(n)+0.1
   elif method == "chirp": a = 0.5+0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*3*t + 0.3*rng.normal())
   elif method == "cosine": a =0.5+0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*t)
   else:
      f1=rng.uniform(1,5); f2=rng.uniform(5,15); ph1=rng.uniform(0,2π); ph2...

      a=np.abs(a)
   return normalize_nonneg(a,S)
```

```
def make_candidate_pool(a,g,k_top):
    n=len(a)
    k_top=max(1,min(k_top,n))
    if 2*k_top >= n: return np.arange(n)
    top = np.argpartition(g, n-k_top)[-k_top:]
    bottom = np.argpartition(g, k_top)[:k_top]
    pool = np.unique(np.concatenate([top,bottom]))
    # ensure includes maybe high mass? no.
    return pool
```

```
def clip_to_objective(a, S, M, margin=1.05):
    cap=np.sqrt(max(1.0, margin*M))
    if a.max() > cap:
       a=np.minimum(a, cap)
       a=normalize_nonneg(a,S)
    return a
```

def clip_coordinates(a, S, target_M):

```python
"""
Local-search solver for

    minimize 2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))**2

 over non-negative sequences.

The implementation normalizes sum(a) = sqrt(2n), so the objective equals
max(conv(a,a)).  It combines:
  - adaptive active-lag weighting with coordinate descent on the weighted
    quadratic surrogate Q_w(a) = sum_t w_t (a*a)_t,
  - many exact two-coordinate moves for Q_w,
  - exact objective checks and monotone polishing,
  - restarts / noise when stuck,
  - optional up-sampling to increase n.

The gradient / quadratic structure used here is for the polynomial square
P(z)^2, where P(z) = sum_j a_j z^j.  For weights w_t,
Q_w = sum_t w_t [z^t] P(z)^2 has gradient
2 * sum_i a_i w_{i+t}, computed by circular correlation with zero padding.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import fft, ifft


DEFAULT_STRATEGIES = [
    ("soft", 8.0, 0.10),
    ("topfrac", 0.50, 0.04),
    ("soft", 20.0, 0.10),
    ("topfrac", 0.25, 0.04),
    ("soft", 50.0, 0.08),
    ("topfrac", 0.05, 0.03),
    ("soft", 120.0, 0.06),
    ("topfrac", 0.01, 0.03),
    ("soft", 300.0, 0.05),
    ("topfrac", 0.002, 0.02),
    ("soft", 1000.0, 0.04),
    ("topfrac", 0.0005, 0.01),
]


def next_power_two(size):
    size = int(size)
    if size <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()


def project_simplex(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(v)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = (css - S) / rho <= u
    idx = np.where(cond)[0]
    if len(idx) == 0:
        return np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    r = int(idx.max())
    theta = (css[r] - S) / float(r + 1)
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = out.sum()
    if s > 1e-300:
        out *= (S / s)
    else:
        out = np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return out


def normalize_nonneg(a, S):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a[a < 0.0] = 0.0
    a[a > 1000.0] = 1000.0
    s = a.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
        a.fill(S / float(len(a)))
    else:
        a *= (S / s)
    return a


def make_initial(n, rng, method="mixed"):
    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    if method == "uniform":
        a = np.ones(n)
    elif method == "random":
        a = 0.1 + rng.random(n)
    elif method == "cosine":
        a = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t)
    elif method == "chirp":
        f = float(rng.uniform(2.0, 8.0))
        a = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * t + float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.28)))
    else:
        f1 = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 4.0))
        f2 = float(rng.uniform(5.0, 12.0))
        f3 = float(rng.uniform(13.0, 30.0))
        p1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.28))
        p2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.28))
        p3 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.28))
        a = (
            0.35
            + 0.25 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * t + p1)
            + 0.20 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f2 * t + p2)
            + 0.15 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f3 * t + p3)
            + 0.10 * rng.random(n)
        )
    a = np.abs(a)
    s = a.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(n)
        s = float(n)
    return a * (S / s)


def downsample_to(a, m):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if m <= 0:
        return a
    if n == m:
        return a.copy()
    if n == 1:
        return np.full(max(1, m), a[0], dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a, left=a[0], right=a[-1])


def upsample_sequence(a, factor=2):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    factor = max(1, int(factor))
    n_new = max(1, n * factor)
    if n == 1:
        return np.full(n_new, a[0], dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new, endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a, left=a[0], right=a[-1])


def load_start_sequence(rng):
    a = None
    try:
        a = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        a = None

    if a is None or a.size == 0:
        return make_initial(8192, rng, "mixed")

    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)) or a.sum() < 1e-8:
        return make_initial(8192, rng, "mixed")

    if a.size > 20000:
        a = downsample_to(a, 16384)

    if a.size < 1024:
        while a.size < 4096 and a.size < 16384:
            a = upsample_sequence(a, 2)

    return a


def compute_convolution(a, N):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    A = fft(a, N)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))[: 2 * n - 1].copy()
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c, A


def safe_objective(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = len(a)
    s = a.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    N = next_power_two(2 * n - 1)
    A = fft(a, N)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))[: 2 * n - 1]
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * c.max() / (s * s))


def final_check(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if a.size <= 20000:
        lst = [float(x) for x in a]
        try:
            val = evaluate_sequence(lst)
            if np.isfinite(val):
                return float(val)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return safe_objective(a)


def make_weights(c, n, model, param, mix):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)

    if model == "soft":
        beta = float(param)
        M = float(c.max())
        if beta <= 0.0 or M <= 0.0:
            base = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
        else:
            tau = max(1e-12, M / beta)
            scaled = (c - M) / tau
            scaled = np.clip(scaled, -40.0, 0.0)
            base = np.exp(scaled)
            s = base.sum()
            if s <= 1e-300:
                base = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
            else:
                base /= s
    elif model == "topfrac":
        frac = float(param)
        K = int(round(frac * (L - 1)))
        K = max(1, K)
        maxK = max(1, (L - 1) // 2)
        K = min(K, maxK)
        if K >= L:
            base = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
        else:
            kth = L - K
            if kth < 0:
                kth = 0
            idx = np.argpartition(c, kth)[kth:]
            if len(idx) > K:
                idx = idx[-K:]
            base = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            base[idx] = 1.0 / float(len(idx))
    else:
        base = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)

    base = 0.5 * (base + base[::-1])

    mix = float(mix)
    if mix > 0.0:
        w = (1.0 - mix) * base + mix * (1.0 / float(L))
    else:
        w = base.copy()

    w = np.maximum(w, 0.0)
    s = w.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    else:
        w /= s
    return w


def gradient_from_A(a, A, w, N):
    W = fft(w, N)
    g = np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[: len(a)]
    return 2.0 * g


def make_candidate_pool(a, g, k_top):
    n = len(a)
    k_top = max(1, min(int(k_top), n))
    if 2 * k_top >= n:
        return np.arange(n)
    top = np.argpartition(g, n - k_top)[n - k_top:]
    bottom = np.argpartition(g, k_top)[:k_top]
    pool = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, bottom)))
    return pool


def pair_candidate_values(src, tgt, a, g, w, abs_cap):
    if len(src) == 0 or len(tgt) == 0:
        return np.empty((0, 0), dtype=np.float64), np.empty((0, 0), dtype=np.float64)

    P = src[:, None]
    Q = tgt[None, :]
    diff = g[src][:, None] - g[tgt][None, :]
    mass_p = a[src][:, None]
    mask = (P != Q) & (mass_p > 1e-12)

    Ap = mass_p.copy()
    if abs_cap is not None and abs_cap > 0.0:
        Ap = np.minimum(Ap, float(abs_cap))

    B = w[2 * P] - 2.0 * w[P + Q] + w[2 * Q]

    shape = diff.shape
    delta = np.zeros(shape, dtype=np.float64)
    epsB = 1e-12

    posB = mask & (B > epsB)
    negB = mask & ~posB

    if np.any(posB):
        d = diff[posB] / (2.0 * B[posB])
        d = np.maximum(d, 0.0)
        d = np.minimum(d, Ap[posB])
        delta[posB] = d

    if np.any(negB):
        delta[negB] = Ap[negB]

    val = -delta * diff + delta * delta * B
    val[~mask] = np.inf

    return val, delta


def find_best_move(a, g, w, rng, k_top, abs_cap):
    n = len(a)
    pool = make_candidate_pool(a, g, k_top)
    src = pool
    tgt = pool

    val, delta = pair_candidate_values(src, tgt, a, g, w, abs_cap)
    flat = val.ravel()
    flat_d = delta.ravel()

    best = None
    finite = np.isfinite(flat)
    if np.any(finite):
        idx = int(np.argmin(flat))
        if flat[idx] < -1e-11:
            row = idx // len(tgt)
            col = idx % len(tgt)
            best = (int(src[row]), int(tgt[col]), float(flat_d[idx]), float(flat[idx]))

    nonzero = np.where(a > 1e-12)[0]
    if len(nonzero) > 0:
        R = min(48, n)
        src_r = nonzero[rng.integers(0, len(nonzero), size=R)]
        tgt_r = rng.integers(0, n, size=R)
        val_r, delta_r = pair_candidate_values(src_r, tgt_r, a, g, w, abs_cap)
        flat_r = val_r.ravel()
        flat_dr = delta_r.ravel()
        finite_r = np.isfinite(flat_r)
        if np.any(finite_r):
            idx_r = int(np.argmin(flat_r))
            if flat_r[idx_r] < -1e-11:
                row = idx_r // len(tgt_r)
                col = idx_r % len(tgt_r)
                cand = (
                    int(src_r[row]),
                    int(tgt_r[col]),
                    float(flat_dr[idx_r]),
                    float(flat_r[idx_r]),
                )
                if best is None or cand[3] < best[3]:
                    best = cand

    return best


def surrogate_coordinate_descent(a_in, w, c, n, N, A_in, S, deadline, rng,
                                 k_top=64, max_moves=200000, abs_cap_factor=50.0):
    a = np.asarray(a_in, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    W = fft(w, N)
    A = A_in if A_in is not None else fft(a, N)
    g = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[:n]

    abs_cap = abs_cap_factor * (S / float(n))
    if abs_cap < 1e-300:
        abs_cap = None

    moves = 0
    last_improve = -1000
    it = 0

    while it < max_moves and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        mv = find_best_move(a, g, w, rng, k_top, abs_cap)
        if mv is None:
            if it - last_improve > 120:
                break
            continue

        p, q, delta, val = mv
        if val >= -1e-10:
            if it - last_improve > 120:
                break
            continue

        oldp = float(a[p])
        delta = min(float(delta), oldp)
        if delta <= 1e-15:
            continue

        newp = oldp - delta
        if newp < 0.0:
            newp = 0.0
            delta = oldp

        g += 2.0 * delta * (w[q:q + n] - w[p:p + n])
        a[p] = newp
        a[q] += delta

        moves += 1
        last_improve = it

        if it % 5000 == 0:
            A = fft(a, N)
            g = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[:n]

    a = normalize_nonneg(a, S)
    return a, moves


def move_new_max(c, a, p, q, d):
    n = len(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1

    start = p if p < q else q
    end = (p if p > q else q) + n
    if end > L:
        end = L

    length = end - start
    if length <= 0:
        return float(c.max())

    tmp = c[start:end].copy()

    off_p = p - start
    tmp[off_p:off_p + n] -= (2.0 * d) * a
    off_q = q - start
    tmp[off_q:off_q + n] += (2.0 * d) * a

    d2 = d * d
    idx = 2 * p
    if 0 <= idx - start < length:
        tmp[idx - start] += d2
    idx = p + q
    if 0 <= idx - start < length:
        tmp[idx - start] -= 2.0 * d2
    idx = 2 * q
    if 0 <= idx - start < length:
        tmp[idx - start] += d2

    localmax = float(tmp.max())
    outmax = -np.inf
    if start > 0:
        outmax = max(outmax, float(c[:start].max()))
    if end < L:
        outmax = max(outmax, float(c[end:].max()))

    return max(localmax, outmax)


def exact_coordinate_descent(a_in, w, c_in, n, N, A_in, S, deadline,
                             k_top=40, max_moves=2000, abs_cap_factor=50.0):
    a = np.asarray(a_in, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    c = np.asarray(c_in, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    A = A_in if A_in is not None else fft(a, N)
    W = fft(w, N)
    g = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[:n]

    current_M = float(c.max())
    abs_cap = abs_cap_factor * (S / float(n))
    if abs_cap < 1e-300:
        abs_cap = None

    obj_tol = max(1e-10, 1e-8 * max(1.0, current_M))
    moves = 0
    last_improve = -1000
    it = 0

    while it < max_moves and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        pool = make_candidate_pool(a, g, k_top)
        if len(pool) == 0:
            break

        val, delta_mat = pair_candidate_values(pool, pool, a, g, w, abs_cap)
        flat = val.ravel()
        flat_d = delta_mat.ravel()
        finite = np.isfinite(flat) & (flat < -1e-10)

        if not np.any(finite):
            if it - last_improve > 60:
                break
            continue

        if int(np.count_nonzero(finite)) > 5:
            temp = np.where(finite, flat, np.inf)
            cand_idx = np.argpartition(temp, 5)[:5]
        else:
            cand_idx = np.where(finite)[0]

        best_cand = None

        for idx in cand_idx:
            row = int(idx) // len(pool)
            col = int(idx) % len(pool)
            p = int(pool[row])
            q = int(pool[col])
            d0 = float(flat_d[idx])
            if d0 <= 0.0:
                continue

            max_d = float(a[p])
            candidates = [d0, 0.5 * d0, 0.25 * d0]
            if d0 < max_d:
                candidates.append(max_d)
            if max_d > 2.0 * d0:
                candidates.append(0.5 * max_d)

            best_d = None
            best_newM = current_M
            for d in candidates:
                d = float(d)
                if d <= 1e-15 or d > max_d:
                    continue
                newM = move_new_max(c, a, p, q, d)
                if newM < best_newM:
                    best_newM = newM
                    best_d = d

            if best_d is not None and best_newM < current_M - obj_tol:
                if best_cand is None or best_newM < best_cand[3]:
                    best_cand = (p, q, best_d, best_newM)

        if best_cand is None:
            if it - last_improve > 60:
                break
            continue

        p, q, d, newM = best_cand
        d = min(float(d), float(a[p]))
        if d <= 1e-15:
            continue

        lin = 2.0 * d
        c[p:p + n] -= lin * a
        c[q:q + n] += lin * a
        d2 = d * d
        c[2 * p] += d2
        c[p + q] -= 2.0 * d2
        c[2 * q] += d2

        g += lin * (w[q:q + n] - w[p:p + n])

        a[p] -= d
        a[q] += d
        if a[p] < 0.0:
            a[p] = 0.0

        current_M = float(newM)
        moves += 1
        last_improve = it

        if it % 1000 == 0:
            c, A = compute_convolution(a, N)
            g = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[:n]
            current_M = float(c.max())

    a = normalize_nonneg(a, S)
    c, A = compute_convolution(a, N)
    return a, c, float(c.max()), moves, moves


def optimize_sequence(a_init, S, n, N, deadline, label, rng):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a_init, S)
    c, A = compute_convolution(a, N)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = float(c.max())

    print(f"[{label}] n={n}, start obj={best_M:.9f}")

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(5.0, deadline - start_time)
    nstrat = len(DEFAULT_STRATEGIES)
    if budget > 1200.0:
        cycles = 4
    elif budget > 500.0:
        cycles = 3
    elif budget > 150.0:
        cycles = 2
    else:
        cycles = 1
    time_per = max(5.0, budget / float(nstrat * cycles))
    surr_time = 0.60 * time_per
    polish_time = 0.40 * time_per

    step_idx = 0
    no_improve = 0
    nstrat = len(DEFAULT_STRATEGIES)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        strat = DEFAULT_STRATEGIES[step_idx % nstrat]
        model, param, mix = strat

        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 2.0:
            break

        w = make_weights(c, n, model, param, mix)

        surr_deadline = min(deadline - 1.0, time.monotonic() + surr_time)
        a2, _ = surrogate_coordinate_descent(
            a, w, c, n, N, A, S, surr_deadline, rng,
            k_top=64, max_moves=500000
        )
        a = normalize_nonneg(a2, S)
        c, A = compute_convolution(a, N)

        polish_deadline = min(deadline - 1.0, time.monotonic() + polish_time)
        a3, c3, M3, _, _ = exact_coordinate_descent(
            a, w, c, n, N, A, S, polish_deadline,
            k_top=40, max_moves=5000
        )
        a = a3
        c = c3
        M = float(M3)

        if M < best_M - 1e-9:
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_M = M
            no_improve = 0
            print(f"[{label}] strategy {step_idx}: obj={M:.9f}  NEW BEST")
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve % max(1, nstrat) == 0:
                print(f"[{label}] strategy {step_idx}: obj={M:.9f}  best={best_M:.9f}")

        if np.isfinite(best_M) and M > best_M * 1.02:
            a = best_a.copy()

        if no_improve > nstrat:
            sigma = (0.015 + 0.01 * min(4, no_improve - nstrat)) * (S / float(np.sqrt(n)))
            base_a = best_a if best_a is not None else a
            a = project_simplex(base_a + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n), S)
            no_improve = 0
            step_idx = 0

        if no_improve > 4 * nstrat:
            break

        step_idx += 1

    c, A = compute_convolution(best_a, N)
    best_M = float(c.max())
    print(f"[{label}] final best obj={best_M:.9f}")
    return best_a, best_M


def choose_best_candidate(candidates):
    best_a = None
    best_o = np.inf
    for a, o in candidates:
        if a is None:
            continue
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            continue
        try:
            o = float(o)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if np.isfinite(o) and o < best_o:
            best_o = o
            best_a = a
    return best_a, best_o


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Main entry point.  Searches for a non-negative sequence with a small
    evaluate_sequence() value and returns the best list found.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    reserve = min(150.0, 0.06 * total_budget)
    main_deadline = t0 + max(30.0, total_budget - reserve)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    a0 = load_start_sequence(rng)
    n = len(a0)
    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    N = next_power_two(2 * n - 1)

    candidates = []

    stage1_deadline = t0 + max(60.0, (main_deadline - t0) * 0.70)
    best_a, best_o = optimize_sequence(a0, S, n, N, stage1_deadline, "stage1", rng)
    candidates.append((best_a, best_o))

    if time.monotonic() < main_deadline - 180.0:
        a_div = make_initial(n, rng, "mixed")
        div_deadline = min(main_deadline - 60.0, time.monotonic() + 150.0)
        best_div, o_div = optimize_sequence(a_div, S, n, N, div_deadline, "diverse", rng)
        candidates.append((best_div, o_div))

    cur_a, cur_o = choose_best_candidate(candidates)

    current_len = len(cur_a)
    while current_len < 16384 and time.monotonic() < main_deadline - 300.0:
        a_up = upsample_sequence(cur_a, 2)
        if len(a_up) > 16384:
            a_up = downsample_to(a_up, 16384)
        n2 = len(a_up)
        S2 = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n2))
        N2 = next_power_two(2 * n2 - 1)

        rem = max(0.0, main_deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem < 150.0:
            break

        stage_deadline = min(main_deadline - 30.0, time.monotonic() + max(120.0, 0.65 * rem))
        best_up, o_up = optimize_sequence(a_up, S2, n2, N2, stage_deadline, f"up{n2}", rng)
        candidates.append((best_up, o_up))

        new_a, new_o = choose_best_candidate(candidates)
        if new_o < cur_o - 1e-9:
            cur_a, cur_o = new_a, new_o
            current_len = len(cur_a)
        else:
            break

    final_a, our_obj = choose_best_candidate(candidates)
    if final_a is None:
        final_a = make_initial(8192, rng, "uniform")
        our_obj = safe_objective(final_a)

    final_a = np.asarray(final_a, dtype=np.float64)
    final_a = np.clip(final_a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    safe_o = safe_objective(final_a)
    final_o = min(our_obj, safe_o)

    checked_o = final_check(final_a)
    if np.isfinite(checked_o):
        final_o = min(final_o, checked_o)

    print(f"Returned: n={len(final_a)}, reported obj={final_o:.9f}")

    return [float(x) for x in final_a]
```
